f2 Lead_muted__ZJh5K" data-container="lead" data-testid="lead" data-search="lead"><p>Use the REST API to interact with GitHub Pages sites and builds.</p></div><div class="MarkdownContent_markdownBody__v5MYy markdown-body pt-3 pb-4"><div class="pb-8"><h2 id="get-a-github-pages-site" tabindex="-1"><a class="heading-link" href="#get-a-github-pages-site">Get a GitHub Pages site<span aria-hidden="true" class="heading-link-symbol"></span></a></h2><div class="d-flex flex-wrap gutter mt-4"><div class="col-md-12 col-lg-6"><div class="RestOperation_codeBlock__mf2k2"><p>Gets information about a GitHub Pages site.</p>
<p>OAuth app tokens and personal access tokens (classic) need the <code>repo</code> scope to use this endpoint.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#get-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Get a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (read)</span></span></li></ul><p>This endpoint can be used without authentication or the aforementioned permissions if only public resources are requested.</p><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-a-github-pages-site--parameters"><a href="#get-a-github-pages-site--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Get a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. The name is not case sensitive.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">repo</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> <span class="color-fg-attention f5 pl-3">Required</span></div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The name of the repository without the <code>.git</code> extension. The name is not case sensitive.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr></tbody></table><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-a-github-pages-site--status-codes"><a   &quot;https_enforced&quot;: true
}</code></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="pb-8"><h2 id="create-a-github-pages-site" tabindex="-1"><a class="heading-link" href="#create-a-github-pages-site">Create a GitHub Pages site<span aria-hidden="true" class="heading-link-symbol"></span></a></h2><div class="d-flex flex-wrap gutter mt-4"><div class="col-md-12 col-lg-6"><div class="RestOperation_codeBlock__mf2k2"><p>Configures a GitHub Pages site. For more information, see &quot;<a href="/github/working-with-github-pages/about-github-pages">About GitHub Pages</a>.&quot;</p>
<p>The authenticated user must be a repository administrator, maintainer, or have the &#x27;manage GitHub Pages settings&#x27; permission.</p>
<p>OAuth app tokens and personal access tokens (classic) need the <code>repo</code> scope to use this endpoint.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="create-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#create-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Create a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (write)</span><span> and </span></span><span><span>&quot;Administration&quot; repository permissions (write)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="create-a-github-pages-site--parameters"><a href="#create-a-github-pages-site--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Create a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. The first line includes the name, type, and whether the parameter is required. The second line is a description of the parameter."><caption class="mt-3 mb-3 h5 float-left">Headers</caption><thead class="visually-hidden"><tr><th scope="col">Name, Type, Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">accept</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> </div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>Setting to <code>application/vnd.github+json</code> is recommended.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr></tbody></table><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. The first line includes the name, type, and whether the parameter is required. The second line is a description of the parameter."><caption class="mt-3 mb-3 h5 float-left">Path parameters</caption><thead class="visually-hidden"><tr><th scope="col">Name, Type, Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">owner</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> <span class="color-fg-attention f5 pl-3">Required</span></div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The account owner of the repository. The name is not case sensitive.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">repo</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> <span class="color-fg-attention f5 pl-3">Required</span></div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The name of the repository without the <code>.git</code> extension. The name is not case sensitive.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr></tbody></table><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. The first line includes the name, type, and whether the parameter is required. The second line is a description of the parameter."><caption class="mt-3 mb-3 h5 float-left">Body parameters</caption><thead class="visually-hidden"><tr><th scope="col">Name, Type, Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">build_type</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> </div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The process in which the Page will be built. <p>The authenticated user must be a repository administrator, maintainer, or have the &#x27;manage GitHub Pages settings&#x27; permission.</p>
<p>OAuth app tokens and personal access tokens (classic) need the <code>repo</code> scope to use this endpoint.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="update-information-about-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#update-information-about-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Update information about a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (write)</span><span> and </span></span><span><span>&quot;Administration&quot; repository permissions (write)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="update-information-about-a-github-pages-site--parameters"><a href="#update-information-about-a-github-pages-site--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Update information about a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. For more about custom domains, see &quot;<a href="https://docs.github.com/pages/configuring-a-custom-domain-for-your-github-pages-site">Using a custom domain with GitHub Pages</a>.&quot;</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">https_enforced</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">boolean</span> </div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>Specify whether HTTPS should be enforced for the repository.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">build_type</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> </div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The process by which the GitHub Pages site will be built. <code>workflow</code> means that the site is built by a custom GitHub Actions workflow. <code>legacy</code> means that the site is built by GitHub when changes are pushed to a specific branch.</p></div><div class="pt-2"><p><span>Can be one of<!-- -->:<!-- --> </span><span><code>legacy</code>,<!-- --> </span><span><code>workflow</code> </span></p></div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">source</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">object</span> </div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>Update the source for the repository. For more information, see &quot;<a href="/github/working-with-github-pages/about-github-pages">About GitHub Pages</a>.</p>
<p>The authenticated user must be a repository administrator, maintainer, or have the &#x27;manage GitHub Pages settings&#x27; permission.</p>
<p>OAuth app tokens and personal access tokens (classic) need the <code>repo</code> scope to use this endpoint.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="delete-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#delete-a-github-pages-site--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Delete a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (write)</span><span> and </span></span><span><span>&quot;Administration&quot; repository permissions (write)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="delete-a-github-pages-site--parameters"><a href="#delete-a-github-pages-site--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Delete a GitHub Pages site&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. This has the same effect as pushing a commit to your default branch, but does not require an additional commit. Manually triggering page builds can be helpful when diagnosing build warnings and failures.</p>
<p>Build requests are limited to one concurrent build per repository and one concurrent build per requester. If you request a build while another is still in progress, the second request will be queued until the first completes.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="request-a-github-pages-build--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#request-a-github-pages-build--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Request a GitHub Pages build&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (write)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="request-a-github-pages-build--parameters"><a href="#request-a-github-pages-build--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Request a GitHub Pages build&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. <p>The authenticated user must have write permission to the repository.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="create-a-github-pages-deployment--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#create-a-github-pages-deployment--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Create a GitHub Pages deployment&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (write)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="create-a-github-pages-deployment--parameters"><a href="#create-a-github-pages-deployment--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Create a GitHub Pages deployment&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. <p>The authenticated user must have read permission for the GitHub Pages site.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-the-status-of-a-github-pages-deployment--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#get-the-status-of-a-github-pages-deployment--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Get the status of a GitHub Pages deployment&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (read)</span></span></li></ul><p>This endpoint can be used without authentication or the aforementioned permissions if only public resources are requested.</p><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-the-status-of-a-github-pages-deployment--parameters"><a href="#get-the-status-of-a-github-pages-deployment--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Get the status of a GitHub Pages deployment&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. <p>The first request to this endpoint returns a <code>202 Accepted</code> status and starts an asynchronous background task to get the results for the domain. After the background task completes, subsequent requests to this endpoint return a <code>200 OK</code> status with the health check results in the response.</p>
<p>The authenticated user must be a repository administrator, maintainer, or have the &#x27;manage GitHub Pages settings&#x27; permission to use this endpoint.</p>
<p>OAuth app tokens and personal access tokens (classic) need the <code>repo</code> scope to use this endpoint.</p></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-a-dns-health-check-for-github-pages--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#get-a-dns-health-check-for-github-pages--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;Get a DNS health check for GitHub Pages&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Administration&quot; repository permissions (write)</span><span> and </span></span><span><span>&quot;Pages&quot; repository permissions (write)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-a-dns-health-check-for-github-pages--parameters"><a href="#get-a-dns-health-check-for-github-pages--parameters">Parameters for &quot;Get a DNS health check for GitHub Pages&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter. The second line is a description of the parameter."><caption class="mt-3 mb-3 h5 float-left">Path parameters</caption><thead class="visually-hidden"><tr><th scope="col">Name, Type, Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">owner</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> <span class="color-fg-attention f5 pl-3">Required</span></div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The account owner of the repository. The name is not case sensitive.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class=""><div class="pl-0 pt-1 pr-1 pb-1"><div><code class="text-bold f5">repo</code> <span class="color-fg-muted pl-2 f5">string</span> <span class="color-fg-attention f5 pl-3">Required</span></div><div class="pl-1 f5 pt-2"><div><p>The name of the repository without the <code>.git</code> extension. The name is not case sensitive.</p></div><div class="pt-0"></div></div></div></td></tr></tbody></table><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="get-a-dns-health-check-for-github-pages--status-codes"><a href="#get-a-dns-health-check-for-github-pages--status-codes">HTTP response status codes for &quot;Get a DNS health check for GitHub Pages&quot;</a></h3><table><thead><tr class="text-left"><th>Status code</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><div><p>OK</p></div></td></tr><tr><td><code>202</code></td><td><div><p>Empty
